cap 61% of the time, and c208 only added Decode-slot waiting
(39622940739, 8352.94).

The Decode cap had two walls:
- DeepGEMM's host leaves SwapAB above 128 tokens per rank. The new image
  39693217189 (built from b24a7a9) adds the SwapAB-144 host patch to the
  2-warpgroup host series.
- FA3 TARGET_VERIFY runs a persistent 132-CTA scheduler with one unit per
  request and kv head. It stays in one round up to 33 requests per rank
  (~48-50 us) and nearly doubles from 34 (89-90 us; kernel bench
  39692814746). Forcing splits only brings 36 down to 75 us.
Estimated against the ~32 ms Decode step (MegaMoE ~18 ms, flat in
tokens; FA3 ~4.5 ms), 33 requests per rank gives about +3% capacity at
almost no step cost. 36 with forced splits nets about the same with more
risk. So the cap goes to 132 first.

What, on top of 3c94c90 (b24a7a9's image line plus fd122bc's replication
state):
- configs/firmus-framework.json:
  - image 39693217189 (sha256 a7ff4c75, 12269379584 bytes);
  - decode_max_running_requests 128 -> 132;
  - decode_cuda_graph_bs gains 33;
  - concurrency 192 -> 196 (132 + 16 extra slots x DP4 = 196 covers every
    request);
  - deep_gemm_overlay_patches gains sm90-swap-ab-geometry-guard.patch (a
    static assert that SwapAB implies the M64/N128 two-warpgroup Decode
    tile; all SASS byte-identical).
- scripts/check-firmus-megamoe.py: SwapAB-144 gate cases (128/129/144/145
  boundary, graph replay, hot experts, mixed geometry with 145 as the
  non-SwapAB side), adapted from d2fd691.
- Tests pin 196 / 132 / graph sizes ending in 32, 33.
Unchanged: 2-warpgroup Prefill (DG_SM90_PREFILL_2WG=1 on Prefill workers),
hot-4 replication (12 redundant Prefill experts), EAGLE3 3/4, SGLang
overlays, KV settings (52 slots x 4224 tokens per rank fit in 393216).

Expected: Decode running ~30 -> ~31 per rank, about +3% throughput with
ITL nearly unchanged. Risk: the new host (SwapAB up to 144) runs in
serving for the first time, but it is gated by the provisioning gate.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>

  request and kv head. It stays in one round up to 33 requests per rank
  (~48-50 us) and nearly doubles from 34 (89-90 us; kernel bench
  39692814746). Forcing splits only brings 36 down to 75 us.
